# Pilates Atlas · Source Verification & Migration Plan

## Scope
- Mat / Small Props: 69 photos
- Apparatus: 117 photos
- Total: 186 original photographs
- Existing Google Doc is a legacy draft, **not** the authoritative exercise source.
- Never label photo-indexed pages as analyzed.

## Required per-photo workflow
1. Open the actual photo; rotate for reading when necessary.
2. Verify the printed page number, English heading, Korean heading, exercise vs. variation.
3. Transcribe/faithfully paraphrase source sections (Why, Starting Position, Breathing, Movement, Watch Points, Variations), distinguishing absent information from present information.
4. Add separate instructor commentary: hip rotation, knee direction, foot contact (heel, ball, arch, sole, or no contact), spinal/pelvic position, hands, shoulder/scapula, gaze.
5. Add concise Inhale/Exhale sequence; identify targets, expected sensation, common compensation, regression, progression, cues, safety.
6. Cross-check text against the photo. Do not infer source claims from filename alone.
7. Save as a uniquely keyed exercise in Pilates Atlas. A photo may contain multiple exercises.
8. Mark status: indexed / photo inspected / analyzed / verified. Only verified counts as completed.

## Navigation
Mat → Reformer → Cadillac → Chair → Barrel → Small Props. Within each apparatus, preserve photographed textbook order. Split pages that contain multiple exercises into independent records while linking their source page.

## Current verified work status (2026-11-07)
- 186 photos indexed and linked in the website.
- Mat 010–030 have teaching summaries but **must still be checked against printed text** before marked verified.
- One Cadillac Double Leg Pump page was actually inspected in a prior pass.
- The 146 generic Google Doc entries are unverified and must not be imported as source-grounded material.
- Public GitHub Pages URL reported 404 by user; publishing must be verified independently.
- **No claim of 186 completed analyses.**

## Quality gates
- No fabricated exercise headings or page numbers.
- English first, Korean alongside.
- Source facts vs. added teaching suggestions clearly labeled.
- Photos upright; no duplicated or mixed apparatus entries.
- Each exercise navigable, searchable, and readable on mobile.
- Website deployment confirmed via HTTP before sharing as accessible.
- Keep one canonical website rather than repeatedly appending drafts to Google Docs.

## 2026-11-13 15:00 KST — Mat 010 Curl Up vs 018 Roll Up direct original-image comparison
- **010 Curl Up**, Drive `16uB1r23FLt4ACxJwXTIGJPjr8hnk6qtt`, printed p.12: original JPEG opened. SOURCE Why: basic abdominal exercise. Start supine neutral spine/pelvis, knees bent pelvis-width, arms relaxed on floor, gaze ceiling. Breath **INHALE Shoulder Flexion → EXHALE Head Nod & Cervical and Upper Thoracic Flexion → INHALE Keep → EXHALE Return to Starting Position**. Movement: transverse abdominis, pelvic floor, rectus abdominis/obliques for spinal flexion, scapular stabilizers. Watch: avoid shoulder/neck tension; maintain neutral pelvis. Variations: Arc Barrel; Circle/Spacer/Ball; both legs in Leg Folding; hands behind head. PHOTO: upper trunk lifts but pelvis remains on mat, knees flexed, feet planted; hand positions vary across photos. Red handwriting appears to cue sternum lowering and looking between thighs, but exact transcription not certified. **Printed/photo verified; handwritten annotations unresolved.**
- **018 Roll Up**, Drive `1atTLtc94l_vKDXJKMNxJk83NhNsIbaRI`, printed p.20: original JPEG opened. SOURCE Why: improve spinal articulation and strengthen abdominals. Start supine neutral spine/pelvis, legs straight pelvis-width, ankles dorsiflexed, arms overhead. Breath **INHALE Shoulder Flexion → EXHALE Roll Up → INHALE Roll Down → EXHALE Starting Position**. Movement: transverse abdominis/pelvic floor, rectus abdominis/obliques, hip flexors, scapular stabilizers. Watch: sequential spinal movement; stabilize shoulder girdle against arm momentum; maintain rounded spinal flexion rather than suddenly straightening. Variations: separate knees, press heels, rise only to thoracic level. PHOTO: torso progresses all the way to sitting/forward flexion with legs extended and heels on mat, then returns to supine. Red annotations include foot/heel and gaze cues, but not independently certified word-for-word. **Printed/photo verified; handwriting unresolved.**
- **Critical distinction:** Curl Up = bent knees, planted feet, only cervical/upper thoracic flexion; Roll Up = straight legs, ankle dorsiflexion, full sequential trunk roll to sitting. These are **not interchangeable**. No invented hip angle or pressure measurement. COACHING (not printed): for Curl Up avoid using neck traction; for Roll Up avoid momentum and forcing lumbar flexion.

- **019 Chair · Tendon Stretch**, printed p.87, Drive `1RPDtrSV9fjzyUAtR1En54bVsAnnIFrCU`: actual original JPEG opened and read. SOURCE WHY: abdominal strengthening, scapular girdle and shoulder stability, hamstring/calf flexibility, upper/lower body balance. START: sit on front edge of Chair, hold chair sides with both hands, feet together on pedal, lift buttocks and flex trunk to a C-curve. BREATH **INHALE Ready → EXHALE Pull Up → INHALE Return to Starting Position**. MOVEMENT: transverse abdominis, pelvic floor; spinal flexion (rectus abdominis and obliques); scapular stabilizers. WATCH: begin with heavy spring and adjust gradually; use lifting strength rather than pulling with hands; maintain gaze to avoid head dropping; control with abdominal rather than arm strength. VARIATION: separate pedals. PHOTO: two side-view phases show hands braced on chair edges, feet contacting pedal via forefoot/toes, pelvis lifting upward with rounded spine; heels not clearly planted. Exact gaze target, hip angles, spring setting, foot pressure unverified. COACHING (not printed): avoid collapsing into shoulders and prioritize a smooth controlled pedal return. STATUS **printed/photo verified; unquantified specifics and coaching not source-certified**.

Variations: Arc Barrel; Circle/Spacer/Ball props; both legs folding; hands behind head.
- **PHOTO:** four frames: supine bent knees, soles on mat; arms initially down, then reaching ceiling; head and upper thorax lift while arms reach along thighs; final supine return.
